- **Country:** `AUS` · **Track:** `OPS`
- **Role:** Winch Operator
- **Scenario:** Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Ocean Trawl & Purse Seine Hydraulic Winch
- **Intro:** Winch Operator — Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Ocean Trawl & Purse Seine Hydraulic Winch — Pre-Start Safety and Area Clearance Verification: No personnel within 2 m of winch drum or wire rope path.
